Adjudicate paper-statistics disagreements for a read-only audit. Independently inspect the supplied paper texts and target locators. You can see shipped and blind values. Decide exact study/outcome/sample/test; a pilot sample may differ from final data, and narrative success cannot be inferred from significance alone. Do not treat a wrong-target blind extraction as a shipped error. Inspect coding_context for documented calculation or coding direction; a documented sign reversal or reproducible transformation may be valid, but do not assume one. Compatible rounding or formatting is shipped_right, not a precision error. A formatted test statistic may validly contain inferred df/N even if those annotations are not printed inline; only positive evidence from design, reference distribution, counts or p-values can establish a wrong annotation. If inference cannot be settled, undeterminable. Give the full corrected test-statistic expression for any established annotation error. For analytic n, distinguish participant/cluster counts from repeated observations in nested or panel models. Different valid sample-level counts alone do not prove a shipped error; if the intended unit or exact critical model is unidentified, return undeterminable and preserve the roles separately. Original primary papers are required for original verification; replication-summary values alone do not suffice. For each item return a JSON object with audit_id (int), field, verdict (shipped_right|blind_right|both_wrong|undeterminable), correct_value (string or null), location, quote (verbatim short evidence occurring in the source text), source_path, reason. Return one JSON array, all items, inside ## Answer. Keep each reason to one or two sentences unless additional detail is essential; quote only the shortest exact supporting span copied from the supplied text, preserving its spelling/OCR. Do not repeat shared background in every row. No markdown prose in the array. If a value is undeterminable, still quote the text showing ambiguity. Preserve bound types in correct_value when relevant. For a reported_success item with a nonempty scope_note, the worker explicitly identified only an overall paper narrative with insufficient individual-effect identification. Return effect_specific_verdict_confirmed true ONLY if neutral locator information uniquely identifies this row and the authors narrative applies specifically to it; otherwise false and undeterminable. Do not transfer a global mixed/successful label to all unidentified outcomes in the article. Explain the scope using a source quotation, never numerical coincidence with the shipped values. For effect sizes, preserve the requested metric; never replace a test statistic with an effect-size coefficient. If a different metric is the only available value and no documented transformation is reproducible, return undeterminable and explain not_comparable. Include correct_metric for ES corrections.
